# Errors on PO files


## Error with polib
We use the po metadata to get the language code and the team, but sometimes it fails, here are the files.

| Package | Lang from file path/name | polib error | Team |
|---------|--------------------------|-------------|------|
 | [lutris]({{< ref "/f28/package/lutris.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f28/lutris/po/pt-br.po | error-os |  | 
 | [moin]({{< ref "/f28/package/moin.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f28/moin/MoinMoin/i18n/pt-br.MoinMoin.po | error-os |  | 
 | [tidy]({{< ref "/f28/package/tidy.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f28/tidy/localize/translations/language_pt_br.po | error-os |  | 
{.sortable}

| Package | Lang from file path/name | Team |
|---------|--------------------------|------|
